# cc-tracking: product requirements

|                      |                                                                                                  |
| -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Status**           | Shipped: the refactor and redesign landed on 2026-11-04                                          |
| **Last updated**     | 2026-11-04                                                                                       |
| **Baseline**         | Everything shipped up to 2026-10-28, including partial payments ([`CHANGELOG.md`](CHANGELOG.md)) |
| **Thai translation** | [`PRD_TH.md`](PRD_TH.md), same IDs in the same order                                             |

## Summary

cc-tracking tracks a small business's company credit cards: where each card is kept, when each
statement closes and must be paid, how much credit is left on accounts that several cards
share, and which statement a purchase lands on. It runs entirely in the browser, in Thai and
English.

The site was refactored and redesigned, shipping on 2026-11-04. This document is the full
context for that work. It says why the app exists, what it must keep doing, the exact rules behind its numbers,
the data that existing browsers and backups already hold, and every decision made along the
way, with the reason. It also says what is free to change, and lists what is still undecided
as [open questions](#open-questions).

## For agents: read this first

1. **Read the whole document before planning.** Then read [`README.md`](README.md) for how
   statements, limits and storage work, and `AGENTS.md` for the stack and conventions.
2. **Know what you may change.** Each section's role in the redesign:

| Section                                              | IDs | In the redesign                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [Business requirements](#business-requirements)      | BR  | Keep. These are why the app exists.                                                      |
| [Functional requirements](#functional-requirements)  | FR  | Keep the behaviour. Where and how it is shown may change.                                |
| [User stories](#user-stories)                        | US  | Walk every story through the new design before building it.                              |
| [Domain rules](#domain-rules)                        | DR  | Keep exactly. Changing one changes the numbers people rely on.                           |
| [Data contracts](#data-contracts)                    | DC  | Keep, or migrate on purpose. Existing browsers, backups and shared links depend on them. |
| [Interface rules](#interface-rules)                  | UI  | Keep. They hold whatever the layout.                                                     |
| [Decisions](#decisions)                              | DD  | Change only on purpose, after reading the reason and the rejected options.               |
| [Out of scope](#out-of-scope)                        | --  | Not built on purpose. Adding one is a new decision.                                      |
| [Current design](#current-design)                    | CD  | Free to change. Listed so a change is a choice, not an accident.                         |
| [Decided changes](#decided-changes-for-the-redesign) | RD  | Build these. They override the rows they name.                                           |
| [Retired](#retired)                                  | --  | Removed on purpose. Don't bring back by accident.                                        |

3. **Settle the open questions with the user first.** Don't guess the answers to
   [open questions](#open-questions); ask, then record the answer here.
4. **Follow the change workflow.** Every change goes through
   `.claude/skills/project-shipping` (worktree, test first, checks, pull request, preview URL)
   and lands through `.claude/skills/project-landing` only after the user approves.
5. **Finish with the [acceptance criteria](#acceptance-criteria-for-the-redesign)** and update
   this document: a changed decision gets a new reason, a retired requirement is marked retired.

IDs are stable: add new ones at the end of their list rather than renumbering, and mark a
retired one as retired rather than deleting it. Each functional requirement names the business
requirements it serves, and each user story names the functional requirements it covers.

Stack: Bun, TypeScript, Astro (static output), Lit, `@kcstyles/reset.css`, Vitest with
happy-dom for tests, static pages on Cloudflare Workers. Commands: `bun run dev`,
`bun run test`, `bun run check`, `bun run build`.

## Problem

The app works, and its rules are settled, but it grew one feature at a time over six weeks.
These call for a redesign:

- **The flows.** How pages lead into one another, and what each page asks of the reader, were
  never designed as a whole. `TODO.md` has carried this since the visual refresh on
  2026-10-27, which changed the look and deliberately left the flows alone.
- **The code.** A refactor alongside the redesign, so the structure matches the product as it
  now is rather than the order it was built in.
- **The look and phone use.** The visual style and how the app works on a phone are part of
  the redesign too.

Pain points the user named (2026-10-29):

- **The dashboard is too busy.** The purchase form, _This sitting_, _Purchases by date_, _Due
  next_ and _Can spend now_ share one page, and what matters is hard to find.
- **Paying is buried.** Recording a payment means hunting through _Due next_ or each card's
  page.
- **The registry is clunky.** Adding and editing cards and limit groups, with their filters and
  dialogs, feels heavy.
- **Actions are split across pages.** Related actions live on different pages, so the user
  keeps jumping between them.

How the app is really used (corrected by the user, 2026-10-30). Purchases are planned, not
entered from receipts, and payments are made whenever room is needed:

- **Buying.** The bookkeeper picks one or more cards and an amount for each, weighing room,
  close date and due date. They send the spender a dated list (card id, name, last 4,
  amount), wait for the spender to say which purchases were made, then reconcile and save.
- **Paying.** The bookkeeper plans payments per card ahead of time, and pays on any day of
  the month, often before a purchase that would go over a limit. A payment can be more than
  is owed, and the app should say so.
- **How often.** Always (more than half of visits): build a purchase list, find a card to
  use, record a payment. Usually (10-40%): change a card's location or limit, check room,
  find cards with something unpaid. Rarely (under 10%): add and set up a card, find where a
  card is kept, back up, change settings. A visit lasts about ten minutes.

## Goals

- **G1. Keep everything that works.** Every requirement, user story, domain rule, data contract
  and interface rule in this document still holds after the redesign, unless it is retired
  here on purpose.
- **G2. Flows designed as a whole**, around the three questions the app answers: what to pay
  and when, how much room is left, and which statement a purchase lands on.
- **G3. Building, sending and confirming a purchase list is quick**, and a list waiting for
  the spender's reply is never lost (RD-09). _(Until 2026-10-30: entering a pile of receipts
  stays at least as fast as today's always-open form; RD-01, RD-02.)_
- **G4. No one loses data.** Data saved in a browser today, and backups saved today, load
  unchanged.
- **G5. A codebase that is easy to change**, keeping the separation between pure rules,
  storage and interface.
- **G6. Fewer page jumps.** Actions that belong to one task sit together, so a task doesn't
  send the user across pages.
- **G7. Checking room and viewing the numbers is fastest on a phone**: which card has credit,
  what is due, and the spending totals, at a glance.
- **G8. Calm to use for a ten-minute visit**: soft colours, plenty of room, and the next thing to
  do shown first (RD-15, RD-21).

## Non-goals of the redesign

- New features that are not in this document. Anything in [Out of scope](#out-of-scope) stays
  out unless decided separately.
- Changing a domain rule or data contract as a side effect of a layout change.
- Moving data to a server or adding accounts (see [Future work](#future-work)).

## Decided changes for the redesign

Settled with the user on 2026-10-30, and all shipped by 2026-11-04. Each overrides the rows it
names elsewhere in this document; those rows were updated or retired as each change shipped. RD-08 to RD-21 were settled later the same day, after the user corrected how the app
is used (see [Problem](#problem)), on a clickable prototype: <https://claude.ai/code/artifact/a290fd13-81d8-483a-9968-8b16e953ca6c> (private).

Phase 1 (rules and storage) shipped on 2026-10-30: the new rules and records sit beside the old ones, and no page uses them yet. See `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-30-redesign-roadmap.md`.

Phase 2 (shell and look) shipped on 2026-10-30: the header, bottom bar, desktop tabs, build marks, the new palette, the early theme and About replacing the footer. Every page still shows today's panels; `/transactions` is a placeholder.

Phase 3 (Home and purchase lists) shipped on 2026-10-31: Home replaced the dashboard with _Needs you_ and _Ready to use_, and four pages (`/list/new`, `/list/draft`, `/list/reply`, `/list/view`) enter, review, reply to and show a purchase list (DD-58). _Due next_ and _Purchases by date_ are off Home until Transactions (phase 4); the card page keeps its Add purchase dialog until phase 5.

Phase 4 (Transactions and payments) shipped on 2026-10-31: Transactions became the ledger (purchases and payments by day, _Upcoming_, search, filter and sort, a _Lists_ view and an _Owed_ view); the payment page `/pay` (the card picker, with the payment sheet over it for a card) is where every way to pay leads, and planned payments join _Needs you_ and the over-the-room warning. Payments convert to card payments at startup, download, copy and restore use backup version 3, and the card page reads the ledger: its statement list has no _Mark paid_ and no per-part undo (DD-76 to DD-96). `/transactions` is no longer a placeholder, and _Due next_ and _Purchases by date_ are back there.

Phase 5 (Cards) shipped on 2026-11-01: the Cards page replaced the registry, with cards by place (or one flat list for any other sort), search, a filter sheet, archived cards folded at the end, and limit group panels whose sheet adds and edits a group (DD-112 to DD-117). The card page was rebuilt around the room, the bill and the statements; a purchase is edited or deleted from its statement row, and the Add purchase dialog is gone (DD-111, DD-118). A card is added and edited on a full screen, `/card/new` and `/card/edit?id=` (DD-114). Storage refuses to delete a card on a draft or a waiting list (DD-119). The old `/cards` query and `?edit=` links are retired (DC-15, DC-16). A feedback round on 2026-11-01 brought back a single purchase without a list (from the card page), listed the payment parts on each statement, gave cards a `name · holder (last4)` title, moved the card page and the edit page under a compact sub-header with Archive and Delete in the card page's More menu, and split the edit page into sections (DD-121 to DD-126). A follow-up the same day moved the payment and list pages under the sub-header too and made a payment that fails after Back say why (DD-124, DD-127).

Phase 6 (Settings) shipped on 2026-11-02: Settings took the redesign's rows in rounded panels, two columns from 900px (CD-11). Locations became stored records, seeded once from the old purchase places, managed at `/settings/locations` (add, rename, delete one with no cards, _Takes purchases_), and a card can be kept at any of them; names show in the reader's language and sort A to Z (DD-128 to DD-132, DD-140). Backup version 3 carries the records as stored and checks each card's location against them; Merge keeps the local records and deletes nothing from a version 3 file (DD-94 retired, DD-131, DD-133, DD-139). Restoring opens a sheet that reads the clipboard first (DD-134). Reset gained _Delete old purchases_, by age, with a count and a second ask, and _Delete all data_ asks inline and seeds the default locations again (FR-32, DD-135, DD-136). The phase 5 leftovers closed: a _Not in a group_ filter, `?newGroup=1`, the build tint before first paint, and token and wording fixes (DD-137, DD-138, DD-143).

Phase 7 (refinement) shipped on 2026-11-04 with the rest of the redesign: one card tile for every card on Home, the new-list picker and Cards (DD-151 to DD-156); limit groups that keep their own colour, chosen in the group sheet (DD-157 to DD-161); every list a row list or a card list, with rows that open in place in _Needs you_, Transactions and the _Lists_ view (DD-162 to DD-165, UI-22); the payment picker and _Owed_ drawn as card tiles (DD-166); statement dates kept only once a statement is fully paid (DD-167); and keyboard focus that survives a row leaving, with the group sheet's choices as radio groups (DD-168).

| RD-09 | **Purchase lists replace receipt entry.** (1) On the new-list picker (`/list/new`, reached from Home's _Ready to use_), tick cards (DD-103). (2) Enter an amount for each card on its own screen, with the purchase date. (3) Review the dated table, with the columns _Card_ (id), _Name (last 4)_ and _Amount_; copy it as text or save it as an image for the chat, then mark it sent. (4) When the spender replies, mark each line _Made_ or _Not made_ and correct the real amount. (5) Save: made lines become purchases on the list's date; not-made lines can move to a draft for the next day. A list is a draft, waiting for the spender, or saved. A waiting list holds its amounts against each group's room until it is saved. A draft can be deleted (_Delete draft_) and a waiting list cancelled (_Cancel list_), which deletes it so it stops holding room; both act at once (DD-74). Each line can carry an optional note, typed under its amount, edited on the reply, and written to the purchase's `note` when the reply is saved (DD-102). _Shipped in phase 3._ _Since 2026-11-01 a single purchase can also be added without a list, from the card page's *Add purchase* (question 13, DD-121); a list is still how a planned purchase goes to the spender._                                                                                                         | RD-01, RD-02, FR-17, FR-18, FR-23, FR-24, US-07, US-08, DD-20 to DD-23, DD-39, DD-40, CD-03 to CD-05                                      |
| RD-10 | **Warnings while entering amounts.** None blocks the list. Each sits under its line with the reason in words, the line gets an amber outline, and a count shows above _Review_. _Over the room_, explaining any room held by a waiting list and any planned payment that would make room, with one action: _Lower to_ the room. _Shared limit_: cards on one list that share a group go over its room together. _Overdue bill_ on the card. _Close date_: the purchase date is the card's close date. _Date has passed_: shown once, at the date, with _Change to today_. Dropped: the future-date warning, since lists are planned ahead, and a warning for a card already on another list. _Shipped in phase 3; the planned-payment part of the over-room explanation shipped in phase 4 (DD-84)._                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | FR-19, FR-20, US-09, DR-11, DD-41                                                                                                         |
| RD-11 | **Payments belong to a card and can be made on any day.** A payment has a card, a date and an amount. Paid money settles the card's oldest statement first, the open period included. Paying more than is owed is accepted and said in words; the extra stays on the card as **credit**, used by its next purchases, so room can go above the limit. Existing per-statement records migrate without changing any total. _Shipped in phase 4._                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | FR-09, FR-12, FR-14, FR-16, US-02, US-25, DR-05 to DR-09, DR-28 to DR-31, DC-02, DC-05 to DC-07, DD-07, DD-37, DD-38, DD-42, known limits |

The user asked for it.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | FR-15, FR-25, US-01, US-13, CD-06, CD-08, DD-43                                                                                           |

Any other build tints the header and the phone's bottom bar in its own colour, marks the tab title `(dev)`, `(local)`, `(pr-<n>)` or `(preview)`, and shows the same mark as plain text right after the brand, in the header's run of brand, mark and date. Needs build stamps for the environment and the branch. _Shipped in phase 2._                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | FR-37, UI-20, DC-11, DD-35, DD-46, CD-02                                                                                                  |

Amber and rose tints mark warnings and overdue, always with words. _Shipped in phase 2; the pastel card chips and the card page's hero on its group's pastel shipped with the Cards page in phase 5 (DD-118, DD-120)._                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | RD-03, G8                                                                                                                                 |

## Background

A small business holds a set of company credit cards. The cards are kept in three places
(Bangkok, Phichit and Krabi), belong to accounts held by three people (KC, NT and RI), and
some accounts share one credit limit across several physical cards. Receipts pile up and are
entered later, in batches. The app has to answer three questions at any moment: what must be
paid and by when, how much can still be spent on each card, and which statement a given
purchase lands on. _(Since 2026-11-04: entering receipts in batches is the old model;
purchases are planned on purchase lists and payments belong to a card, see [Problem](#problem),
RD-09 and RD-11.)_

The data is small: about a dozen cards and a few hundred purchases a year. Several choices
below (full scans at startup, loading every purchase on a page) rely on that.

### Users

- **Bookkeeper** -- enters receipts in batches, marks statements paid, and reconciles the app
  against bank statements. The main user. Often interrupted halfway through a pile. _(Since
  2026-11-04: they plan purchases on purchase lists and record payments to a card rather than
  marking statements paid, see [Problem](#problem), RD-09 and RD-11.)_
- **Card administrator** -- keeps the card registry right: adds cards, sets billing cycles,
  limit groups, locations and holders, archives cards that are retired. Often the same
  person as the bookkeeper.
- **Spender** -- needs to know, before buying, which card has room and when that purchase
  will be due.

## Business requirements

| ID    | Requirement                                                                                                                     |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| BR-01 | Never miss a card payment: show what is due next across every card, and how urgent it is.                                       |
| BR-02 | Know how much can still be spent on each card, counting limits that several cards share.                                        |
| BR-03 | Record every purchase against the right card and the statement the bank will put it on.                                         |
| BR-04 | Make entering a pile of receipts fast and resumable after an interruption.                                                      |
| BR-05 | Keep an accurate registry of cards: where each is kept, who holds it, its billing cycle and its limit group.                    |
| BR-06 | Keep settled history stable: paying a statement freezes it against later changes to the card.                                   |
| BR-07 | Record what happened, not police it: over-limit, future-dated and close-date purchases are saved with a warning, never refused. |
| BR-08 | Data stays private to the user's own browser, and the user can back it up, move it and restore it without losing anything.      |
| BR-09 | Work in Thai and English, with dates and amounts that match Thai bank statements.                                               |
| BR-10 | Work on a phone as well as a desktop.                                                                                           |
| BR-11 | Control which places new purchases may be entered from, without editing every card.                                             |
| BR-12 | Report spending over a period: how many purchases, how much, on which cards.                                                    |

## User stories

Each story is followed by its acceptance criteria.

### Paying on time

**US-01 -- See what is due next.** As a bookkeeper, I want one list of what each card owes
next, so that I never miss a due date.

- Each unarchived card appears once, with the oldest closed unpaid statement that has money
  on it, or the open period when nothing is owed.
- Overdue rows say how many days overdue; rows due within 7 days are marked as due soon.
- The list sorts by card, location, closes, due or total. _(Since 2026-11-04: the *Owed* view sorts by most urgent, card, due date or most owed, RD-13.)_
- _Covers FR-15, FR-36._

**US-02 -- Mark a statement paid.** As a bookkeeper, I want to mark a statement paid from the
dashboard or the card page, so that the due list and available credit move on.

- Only a closed statement can be marked paid.
- The payment dialog starts at what is left and today, so paying in full is one confirm.
- The first payment freezes the statement's close and due dates. _(Since 2026-11-04: once the statement is fully paid, DD-167.)_
- Available credit on the card's group rises by exactly the amount paid.
- The card page can unmark each payment. _(Since 2026-11-04: a payment belongs to the card and is made at `/pay`; there is no per-part *Unmark*, and a wrong payment is deleted from Transactions, RD-11, DD-77.)_
- _Covers FR-09, FR-14._

**US-03 -- Trust settled history.** As a bookkeeper, I want a paid statement to keep its dates
when the card's cycle is edited later, so that past reconciliations stay true.

- After a cycle edit, paid statements show the dates they were paid against.
- _Covers FR-14._

**US-25 -- Pay a statement in parts.** As a bookkeeper, I want to record each payment toward a
statement as it happens, so that a bill paid 40,000 one day and 60,000 another is tracked
exactly.

- Each payment has its own amount and date, and is listed on the card page with its own
  _Unmark_. _(Since 2026-11-04: payments are card payments made at `/pay`, listed as parts on
  each statement with no *Unmark*; a wrong payment is deleted from Transactions, RD-11, DD-77.)_
- The statement shows what was paid and what is left; it counts as paid once nothing is left.
- _Due next_, the card page's _Next due_ and _Owed_, and the group's available credit all use
  what is left.
- Unmarking the last payment returns the statement to unpaid.
- _Covers FR-12, FR-14, FR-15, FR-16._

### Knowing how much is left

**US-04 -- See room on every card.** As a spender, I want to see how much each card can still
take and when a purchase made today would be due, so that I pick the right card.

- _Ready to use_ on Home (RD-15, since phase 3) lists only unarchived cards that can take a purchase.
- Each row shows available out of limit, how much of the limit is used in words and as a bar,
  and the open period's close and due dates.
- A card sharing its limit says how many others share it.
- _Covers FR-11._

**US-05 -- Share one limit across cards.** As a card administrator, I want several cards on
one account to draw on one limit, so that the room shown is the account's real room.

- Cards pointing at the same group show the same available amount.
- Purchases on any of them, archived ones included, reduce it.
- _Covers FR-08, FR-09, FR-05._

**US-06 -- Find cards with no limit.** As a card administrator, I want to know which cards have
no limit group, so that I can assign them.

- The dashboard counts them and links to the registry; the card list can filter to "not
  assigned". _(Since phase 5 the link opens the Cards page, which has no "not assigned"
  filter; a card with no group shows `—` where its room would be. Since phase 6 the link opens
  `/cards?ungrouped=1`, the Cards filter's *Not in a group*, DD-137.)_
- The card form will not save without a group, and its group list is disabled until a group
  exists.
- _Covers FR-06, FR-08, FR-11._

### Entering receipts

**US-07 -- Build a purchase list quickly.** As a bookkeeper, I want to tick cards, enter amounts, review the list and mark it sent, so that I can plan a day's buying without extra steps.

- On Home I tick cards under _Ready to use_, then enter an amount for each on one screen. _(Since 2026-11-04: cards are ticked on the new-list picker, `/list/new`, which Home's *Ready to use* leads to, DD-103.)_
- Review shows the dated table; I copy it as text or save it as an image, then mark it sent.
- A failed save keeps what I typed and says why.
- _Covers FR-17, FR-18, FR-23 (retired; RD-09 replaces it)._

**US-08 -- Pick up where I left off.** As a bookkeeper who gets interrupted, I want a list waiting for the spender to stay on Home until its reply is saved, so that nothing is lost after an interruption.

- A list waiting for the spender, and any draft, shows under _Needs you_ on Home.
- It survives a reload, closing the tab and the next day.
- Saving the reply turns made lines into purchases and removes it from _Needs you_.
- _Covers FR-24 (retired; RD-09 replaces it)._

**US-09 -- Be warned, not blocked.** As a bookkeeper, I want warnings when an amount is unusual, so that I can check it without being stopped from sending the list.

- Over the room: amber outline with the reason under the line, and one action, _Lower to_ the room.
- On the close date, on an overdue bill and on a past date: amber warnings with the reason in words.
- There is no future-date warning, since lists are planned ahead.
- _Covers FR-19, FR-20; met by RD-10._

**US-10 -- Fix a purchase.** As a bookkeeper, I want to edit or delete a purchase on the card
page, so that a typo doesn't need delete-and-re-add.

- _Edit_ opens the form filled in, titled "Edit purchase", with the card fixed.
- Saving keeps the purchase id and its original entry time; a changed date moves it to the
  right statement.
- Works on archived cards too.
- _Covers FR-21._

**US-11 -- Control where purchases are entered.** As a card administrator, I want to choose
which places take new purchases, so that cards kept elsewhere can't be picked by mistake.

- Settings lists Bangkok, Phichit and Krabi as checkboxes; Krabi is on by default.
- Only cards in ticked places appear in the purchase form and in _Can spend now_.
- With nothing ticked, the form and Settings say so and say how to fix it.
- _(Since phase 6: Settings › Locations lists every location with a *Takes purchases* tick,
  Krabi on by default; only cards in ticked places appear under *Ready to use* and on the
  new-list picker; with nothing ticked the Locations page warns that *Ready to use* is empty,
  and Home and the card page link to it, RD-17, DD-142.)_
- _Covers FR-22._

**US-12 -- Stay correct past midnight.** As a bookkeeper who leaves the tab open, I want the
default date to follow the real day, so that tomorrow's receipts aren't dated yesterday.

- After midnight the default date and the date presets move to the new day.
- A date I picked by hand is left alone.
- _Covers FR-26._

### Reporting

**US-13 -- Summarise spending over a period.** As a bookkeeper, I want purchases counted and
totalled for a range of dates, so that I can report what was bought then.

- Presets: Today, This week (Monday to Sunday), This month (the default), Last month, Custom.
- Shows count, total and cards used; can filter to one card.
- Filters on purchase date, not entry time; archived cards are included and tagged.
- The detail list opens on request, grouped by day, each card linked.
- _Covers FR-25._

**US-14 -- Review one card's history.** As a bookkeeper, I want a card's statements with their
purchases, so that I can reconcile against the bank statement.

- Newest first, the current statement marked; quiet months fold into one line.
- The page shows available credit, what is owed and the next due date.
- _Show older statements_ pages further back, 12 periods at a time.
- _Covers FR-12, FR-16._

### Managing cards

**US-15 -- Register a card.** As a card administrator, I want to add a card with its cycle,
location, group and holder, so that its statements are worked out for me.

- Id must be unique and cannot change after saving; last 4 must be four digits.
- Cycle is either "due N days after closing" or "due on a fixed day".
- A supplementary card asks for its own cardholder.
- _Covers FR-01 to FR-04._

**US-16 -- Find a card fast.** As a card administrator, I want to search, filter and sort the
card list, so that I can find a card among many.

- Search matches name, id, last 4 and comment.
- Filters: cardholder, location, limit group (including not assigned). _(Since phase 5:
  *Kept in* and *Account holder*, DD-116.)_
- The view is kept in the address, so a reload or shared link keeps it.
- _Covers FR-06._

**US-17 -- Retire a card.** As a card administrator, I want to archive a card, so that it leaves
daily views but keeps its history.

- Archived cards leave the dashboard and purchase form, appear in an "Archived (N)" list, and
  still count against their group.
- Unarchive brings one back. Delete is offered only for a card with no purchases.
- _Covers FR-05._

**US-18 -- Keep the registry tidy.** As a card administrator, I want to add and edit cards and
groups without the page growing, and fold the lists I am not using, so that the registry stays
short on a phone.

- Add and Edit open a dialog; a failed save keeps the dialog open.
- Each list remembers whether I left it open.
- From a card's page, _Edit card_ opens its dialog in the registry.
- _(Since phase 5 a card has a full edit screen and a group a sheet over Cards; only the
  archived cards fold; the card page's *Edit* opens `/card/edit?id=`, DD-112 to DD-114.)_
- _Covers FR-07, FR-38._

### Data safety

**US-19 -- Back up my data.** As a bookkeeper, I want to save a copy of everything as a file or
as text, so that clearing the browser doesn't lose my records.

- _Download a copy_ (its _Download_ button) saves indented JSON named `cc-tracking-<date>.json`;
  _Copy as text_ (its _Copy_ button, which reads _Copied_ after) puts compact JSON on the
  clipboard. _(Until phase 6: *Download file* and *Copy text*.)_
- The copy button is hidden where the browser can't write the clipboard.
- _Covers FR-28, FR-29._

**US-20 -- Restore or move my data.** As a bookkeeper, I want to restore a copy from a file or
pasted text, choosing to merge or replace, so that I can move between browsers safely.

- If the browser already has records, I see both sets of counts and choose Merge or Replace.
- Replace is shown as the dangerous choice.
- A malformed copy is rejected before anything is written, naming the record and the problem.
- _Covers FR-30, FR-31._

**US-21 -- Start over.** As a bookkeeper, I want to delete all data after a clear confirmation,
so that I can start clean.

- _Delete…_ asks in place: it says how many of each record go (cards, purchases, payments,
  limit groups, lists) and that it can't be undone; _Keep my data_ backs out, _Delete everything_
  (danger) deletes.
- My language and theme are kept, and the warning says so.
- Afterwards the default locations are back, so Home and the Locations page aren't empty.
- _Covers FR-32._

**US-22 -- Know when a save failed.** As a bookkeeper, I want a clear error when a write fails,
so that I never think something saved when it didn't.

- The banner names what failed and stays after the page re-reads its data.
- _Covers FR-28, FR-33._

### Language and devices

**US-23 -- Use the app in Thai or English.** As any user, I want to pick my language in
Settings without losing what I typed, so that I can work in the language I read best.

- The choice is remembered. Dates use Gregorian years in both languages so they match bank
  statements. My own notes and names are never translated.
- _Covers FR-34, FR-35._

**US-24 -- Use the app on a phone.** As a spender, I want the app usable on a phone, so that I
can check room before buying.

- Panels stack, every table stays readable at phone width, and the theme follows the device
  unless I pick Light or Dark.
- _Covers FR-27, FR-36._

## Domain rules

The exact rules behind every number the app shows. Each is covered by tests in
`tests/lib/domain/`, for the code in `src/lib/domain/`; a refactor should keep those tests passing unchanged.

| ID    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DR-01 | **Money** is stored as whole satang (1 baht = 100 satang). Typed amounts take digits with up to two decimals, commas ignored, and must be above zero; limits follow the same rule. Shown as `฿1,234.56` in both languages.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| DR-02 | **Dates** are plain `YYYY-MM-DD` strings in Asia/Bangkok. Reading the clock to get today is the only place a time zone enters; all date arithmetic uses `Date.UTC` and `getUTC*`, never local-time `Date`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| DR-03 | **A statement period** is named `YYYY-MM` after the month its close date falls in, and runs from the day after the previous close to the close date, inclusive. A purchase on the close date belongs to that statement, not the next.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| DR-04 | **Due dates.** _Offset_: close date plus N days. _Fixed_: day M of the month; if M is not later than the close day, the due date is in the next month. A close or due day past the end of a month becomes its last day.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| DR-05 | **Statements are derived on read.** Only payments are stored, one record per card and period, holding every part paid toward that statement (DR-28). The first part freezes the close and due dates; later parts keep them. _(Since 2026-11-04 the dates freeze when the statement is fully paid, not at the first payment; until then it follows the card's cycle, DD-167.)_ _(Since 2026-11-04: payments belong to a card, `cc:cardpayment:<card>:<id>`, and settle the oldest statement first, the open period included; the kept dates are their own record, kept once a statement is fully paid, RD-11, DD-167.)_ |
| DR-06 | **What is due next** for a card: walking from the period of its earliest purchase up to the open period, the first closed statement that is unpaid and has a total above zero; if there is none, the open period.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| DR-07 | **Urgency**, in order: _future_ while today is on or before the close date; _open_ if paid or the total is zero; _overdue_ once past the due date; _soon_ within 7 days of it; otherwise _open_. A period with no spending can never be overdue. A _future_ statement cannot be marked paid.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DR-08 | **The open period** is the statement a purchase made today would land on. The card page marks it _Current_.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| DR-09 | **Available credit** for a group is its limit less, on every card in the group (archived ones included), what is left on each closed statement plus the whole of the open period. The open period counts in full even if stored data holds a payment against it. It can go below zero. Each payment gives back exactly its amount. _(Since 2026-11-04: a group's available credit is its limit less what its cards owe, less their credit; payments belong to a card and settle the oldest statement first, the open period included, RD-11.)_                                                                         |
| DR-10 | **Can spend now** shows the open period's close and due dates (DR-08), not what is due next (DR-06). On a card with an overdue statement the two differ, and both are right for their question. _Applies to *Ready to use* since phase 3._                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| DR-11 | **Over-limit note.** Adding a purchase above the available credit saves it and names the amount over and the group. Editing a purchase never shows the note, because available credit already counts the old amount.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| DR-12 | **Who can take a purchase**: an unarchived card whose location is in the Settings list. The default list is Krabi only. An empty list is a real answer (nobody can), not "unset". _(Since phase 6 the list is the location records ticked *Takes purchases* (RD-17); the default records tick Krabi only, and none ticked still means nobody.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| DR-13 | **Owners.** A group's account holder is KC, NT or RI; a missing or unknown value reads as KC. A card's cardholder is its own owner if it is supplementary, otherwise its group's account holder, otherwise nobody (no group). A group is labelled `Name (OWNER)`, untranslated.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| DR-14 | **Locations** are `bangkok`, `phichit` and `krabi`; the default is `bangkok`. _(Since phase 6 those are the ids of the three default records, and any other stored record is a location too, its id made from its name (DD-51). A new card starts at `bangkok` when that record exists, else the first A to Z (DD-140).)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| DR-15 | **Entry time** (`addedAt`) is set when a purchase is first saved, from the dashboard or the card page, and an edit keeps it. Purchases saved before 2026-10-27 have none and are never backfilled.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| DR-16 | _(Retired in phase 3 with *This sitting*; replaced by waiting lists on Home, RD-15.)_ _**This sitting**_ holds purchases whose entry time is at or after the batch start, latest entered first. On the very first load the batch start is set to that moment, so the list starts empty.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| DR-17 | **Date ranges** filter on the purchase date, not the entry time. Weeks run Monday to Sunday. The default preset is This month. A custom range typed backwards is turned the right way round. Within a day, the latest entered comes first.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| DR-18 | **Card page history** counts back from the later of the open period and the period of the latest purchase, so a future-dated purchase still has a statement to edit it from.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DR-19 | **Identity and deletion.** A card id is unique and never changes. A card can be deleted only while it has no purchases (otherwise archive it). A group can be deleted only while no card uses it. (Storage enforces both now, and the UI still hides the buttons; see DC-13.)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| DR-20 | **Not assigned** means an unarchived card with no group, or with a group id that matches no group. The card form requires a group. A not-assigned card is left out of _Can spend now_ until it is given one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DR-21 | **Card page paging**: 12 periods are shown, and _Show older statements_ adds 12 more.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| DR-22 | **Quiet months**: consecutive statements that are closed, empty and unpaid fold into one line. The open period and a paid empty statement never fold. The first is where the next purchase lands; a paid empty statement is kept as a rule only: since phase 4 a statement is paid only with money paid against it, so one with a total of zero stays unpaid and folds like any other quiet month. A statement with purchases starts expanded.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| DR-23 | **Usage**: the share of the limit used, clamped to 0--100%. Amber from 80%; red only when used is greater than the limit. A zero limit reads as full once anything is spent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DR-24 | **"Shared with N more"** counts every other card in the group, archived ones included.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| DR-25 | **Cycle inputs**: close day and due day 1 to 31, days until due 1 to 60.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| DR-26 | **Last 4** is exactly four digits in the card form.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| DR-27 | **Relative dates** in _Can spend now_ read "today", "in N days" or "N days ago". A past date there is never called overdue; overdue is the due list's word. _Applies to *Ready to use* since phase 3._                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| DR-28 | **Payments in parts.** A statement's paid amount is the sum of its parts; what is left is the total less that, never below zero, so paying more than is left is accepted and leaves nothing owed. A statement is paid when a payment record exists and nothing is left. A partly paid statement is unpaid for DR-06 and DR-07, so it stays due and can become overdue.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| DR-29 | **Old payment records.** A record without `parts` (saved before 2026-10-28) reads as one part for the statement's whole total, dated its `paidAt`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| DR-30 | **Part order and undo.** Parts are kept oldest first, and the record's `paidAt` is the latest part's date. Unmarking removes one part; removing the last one deletes the record. _(Since 2026-11-04: there is no per-part undo; a payment belongs to a card and a wrong one is deleted from Transactions, and a statement's dates are kept once it is fully paid, RD-11, DD-77, DD-167.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| DR-31 | **Payment input.** The amount follows DR-01 (above zero, up to two decimals); the date must be a real date.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## Data contracts

What existing browsers, backup files and links already contain. A refactor that changes any of
these needs a migration, or it loses or misreads someone's data.

| ID    | Contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DC-01 | **Repository.** Storage is reached only through the `Repository` interface, made of key lookups and prefix scans. Every implementation must pass the shared contract suite (`src/lib/storage/contract.ts`). `src/lib/domain` imports nothing from storage or the interface, and components get data as properties and send events; none imports `#lib/storage`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| DC-02 | **Record keys** in `localStorage`: `cc:card:<id>`, `cc:purchase:<cardId>:<date>:<uuid>`, `cc:payment:<cardId>:<period>`, `cc:limitgroup:<id>`, `cc:cardpayment:<cardId>:<id>`, `cc:statementdates:<cardId>:<period>`, `cc:list:<id>`, `cc:location:<id>`. Phase 4 adds no key. Each segment is `encodeURIComponent`-encoded. Deleting a card also removes its card payments and statement dates; it scans `<encoded id>:` with the trailing colon, so it never touches a card whose id merely starts with the same text. _(Since 2026-11-03 no new record key.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| DC-03 | **Other keys**: `cc:settings`, `cc:batch-start` (start of _This sitting_, an ISO instant; no longer read or written since phase 3, a leftover stays until reset, DD-14), `cc:lang` (`en` or `th`), `cc:theme` (theme pick), `cc:panel:<name>` (`open` or `closed`; since phase 5 only `cards-archive`, the Cards page's archived fold, DD-113; the old `cards`, `cards-archived` and `limit-groups` are no longer read, and a leftover stays until reset), `cc:migration:location` (cards reset by the location migration), `cc:migration:location-records` (`done` once the default location records are seeded, since phase 6, DD-128), `cc:migration:payments` (`done` once stored payments are converted to card payments; written at startup since phase 4, DD-80, and by Replace on restore, DD-93), `cc:probe` (storage check at startup). A failed read or write of language, theme or panel state falls back to the default and never breaks the page. _(Since 2026-11-03 `cc:migration:group-colors`, `done` once every stored limit group has a colour; written at startup, DD-158.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| DC-04 | **Reset** removes cards, purchases, payments, limit groups, card payments, statement dates, lists, locations, settings, the batch start and every `cc:migration:` key (any pending migration notice, the payments marker and, since phase 6, the location seed marker). It keeps language, theme and panel state, so the page doesn't change under the reader. _Since phase 6 Settings seeds the default locations again right after *Delete all data*, Krabi taking purchases, so Home and the Locations page are not empty (DD-128)._                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DC-05 | **Backup format**, version 3 _(version 2 until phase 4, DD-15; replaced by DD-93)_: `{ version: 3, exportedAt, limitGroups, cards, purchases, payments, statementDates, lists, locations }`, where `payments` are card payments (with their optional `note`, DD-101) and `locations` carry `takesPurchases`. The batch start, language, theme and panel state are never in a backup. Download, copy and restore all use it. A file of version 2 is converted on the way in (payments with their `parts` become card payments, its optional `settings` give the locations, and it has no lists); any other version is rejected. Since phase 6 export writes the location records as stored, or the default locations while none is stored (DD-139), and import never writes settings (DD-94 retired). The download is named `cc-tracking-<exportedAt date>.json`. _(Since 2026-11-03 each limit group in `limitGroups` carries its `color`, one of twelve names, when it has one; the version stays 3, DD-157, DD-159.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DC-06 | **Restore checks shapes and ranges**: valid JSON, version 2 or 3, the lists of that version present (a version 3 file also needs its statement dates, lists and locations, each record checked, and a list line's `actual`, `made` and `note`); each record an object; ids, names, `last4` and `cardId` present; owner in its closed set; a card's location in the closed set in a version 2 file, and among the file's own location ids in a version 3 file (since phase 6, `backup.problem.unknownLocation`); cycle kind known with whole-number days; dates valid `YYYY-MM-DD`; amounts and limits whole numbers; `addedAt` a valid instant if present; a card payment's or list line's `note`, if present, text (DD-101, DD-102); a payment's `parts`, if present, a non-empty list of valid dates and whole-satang amounts above zero. _Since phase 1 (RD-06) it also checks ranges, as the forms do:_ purchase, card payment and list line amounts, a line's `actual` and a group's limit above zero; `last4` exactly four digits (DR-26); close day and due day 1 to 31 and days until due 1 to 60 (DR-25); a card payment's status `planned` or `paid`, a list's status `draft`, `sent` or `saved`, a line's `made` and a location's `takesPurchases` true or false. A version 2 file's payments are checked for shape only, then converted. It does not check `limitGroupId`, `archived`, `comment` or `supplementary`, that a `cardId` names a card in the file, or that a due date falls after its close date. Unknown places inside `settings` are dropped, and a `settings` that is not an object is ignored. Nothing is written when any record is bad. Restore then writes groups, cards, purchases and payments (and, from version 3, statement dates, lists and locations) in that order. It is not atomic. _Since phase 4:_ counts in the confirmation include lists; Merge and Replace keep their meaning (DD-17) and Replace sets `cc:migration:payments`; import removes stale `m:` parts before converted ones (DD-95). _Since phase 6:_ import no longer writes settings (DD-94 retired); Merge skips a file's location whose id is already stored (DD-131); stale `m:` parts are removed only for a file converted from version 2, so a Merge of a version 3 file deletes nothing (DD-95, DD-133); after the write the default locations are seeded if none is stored and every card naming no stored record is repaired (DD-128, DD-130). A card whose group is not in the file is still imported. _(Since 2026-11-03 a group's `color` is not checked: missing or unknown, the file still restores. Merge keeps the stored group's colour in that case, and Merge and Replace give every group still without one a colour before they finish, DD-159.)_ |
| DC-07 | **Record shapes** (`src/lib/domain/types.ts`): `Card { id, name, last4, location, cycle, comment?, archived, supplementary?, owner?, limitGroupId? }`, where `location` is a location record's id (any record since phase 6, RD-17); `LocationRecord { id, name, takesPurchases }`; `CycleRule` is `{ kind: "offset", closeDay, dueOffsetDays }` or `{ kind: "fixed", closeDay, dueDay }`; `Purchase { id, cardId, date, amount, note, addedAt? }`; `StatementPayment { cardId, period, paidAt, closeDate, dueDate, parts? }` with `PaymentPart { paidAt, amount }`; `LimitGroup { id, name, limit, owner? }`; `CardPayment { id, cardId, date, amount, status, period?, note? }` where `status` is `planned` or `paid`, `period` is set only on records converted from the old model, and `note` is absent when none was written (DD-101); `PurchaseList { id, date, status, lines, sentAt?, savedAt?, movedTo? }` where `status` is `draft`, `sent` or `saved`, with `ListLine { cardId, amount, made?, actual?, note? }`, whose `note` is trimmed and absent when blank (DD-102). A backup's notes (payments and list lines) are trimmed on import and a blank one dropped, so the rule holds for restored data too. Purchase and group ids are random UUIDs. Amounts and limits are satang.
