Purchases saved from a spender's reply have ids `<list id>-<n>`. _(Since 2026-11-03 `LimitGroup { id, name, limit, owner?, color? }`, where `color` is one of `blue, green, purple, orange, teal, rose, amber, indigo, olive, cyan, plum, slate`, DD-157.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| DC-08 | **Settings record** `cc:settings` is `{ purchaseLocations: Location[] }`. An empty list is kept as written; a missing or garbled record falls back to `["krabi"]`. _(Since phase 6 the app no longer writes it, and reads it only to seed the location records once (DD-128); a version 2 file's `settings` still gives its locations on import (DC-05). A stored one stays until reset.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| DC-09 | **Stale fields.** Cards stored before 2026-09-24 carry an `owner` from when the owner was on the card; `Card.owner` is read only when `supplementary` is set, so those values are ignored. Some cards carry a per-card purchase flag from before purchase places moved to Settings; nothing reads it. Keep both guards, and don't reuse those field names for something else.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| DC-10 | **Location migration** runs at every startup, is safe to run twice, resets unknown locations to Bangkok, and records which cards it reset for a one-time notice. A failure on one card does not stop the page loading. _(Since phase 6 it checks each card against the stored location records, after the seed, at startup and after a restore: a missing default record comes back, any other unknown value moves the card to the first record A to Z (DD-130).)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| DC-11 | **Build stamps.** `PUBLIC_COMMIT_SHA`, `PUBLIC_BUILT_AT` and `PUBLIC_PR_NUMBER` are stamped at build time (`astro.config.ts`) and read through `import.meta.env`. `PUBLIC_PR_NUMBER` is always set, empty outside previews. `PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT` (`local`, `preview` or `production`; `local` when unset at build) and `PUBLIC_BRANCH` (the branch built) are stamped too, and always set. The dev server reads as `development` with no stamps, whatever the environment holds. _(Named `BUN_PUBLIC_*` before DD-170.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| DC-12 | **Purchase keys contain the date.** Saving a purchase first removes any key with the same card and id under any date, so an edit that changes the date moves the record rather than copying it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| DC-13 | **Repository deletes guard.** Storage refuses to delete a card that has purchases, a limit group a card uses, or a location a card is kept at (RD-07, shipped in phase 1 and guarded in the repository contract suite). Since phase 5 it also refuses a card on a draft or on a list waiting for the spender (DD-119); a saved list doesn't block it. `deleteCard` still removes the card's payments, card payments and statement dates. The interface still hides the buttons as well.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DC-14 | **Stored order.** Cards and groups are listed in key order (by encoded id). The _Saved order_ sort shows exactly that. _(Since 2026-11-04: there is no *Saved order* sort; Cards sorts by place, room, name or close, RD-16.)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| DC-15 | _(Retired in phase 5, question 15: replaced by DD-115. The old keys are no longer read.)_ **Address query** on `/cards`, which shared links depend on: cards `q`, `owner`, `loc`, `group` (a group id or `none`), `sort`, `dir`; groups `gq`, `gowner`, `gsort`, `gdir`. Defaults are left out. The address is replaced, not pushed, so typing doesn't fill the Back history.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| DC-16 | _(Retired in phase 5, question 15: the edit screen has its own address, `/card/edit?id=<id>`, DD-114.)_ **Deep link** `/cards?edit=<id>` opens that card's edit dialog. It is read on first load only, and an unknown id is ignored.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| DC-17 | **Old address.** `/backup` redirects to `/settings`. Keep a redirect for any page address a redesign retires.
