Keys in the markup keep one mechanism, keep addresses and the setting as they are, and leave the copy visible to Container tests.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Per-locale pages (`/th/...`): the address would fight the setting with a redirect on every load, double every address and make every link locale-aware, for no gain on pages that are `noindex` and need the script anyway. Text set only in each component's script: translation wiring in every component, and markup with no copy to test. |
| DD-172 | **Static first, Svelte islands for what the browser renders.** A component is static `.astro` wherever its output is the same for every reader (structure, headings, labels, links, empty states). What is built from the reader's data or reacts to input (lists, forms, sheets, filters) is a Svelte 5 component (`@astrojs/svelte`) in the same atomic tier, hydrated as an island (`client:load`, or `client:visible` below the fold), kept as small as the changing part allows. Its build-time render is the empty frame and it reads the repository only in the browser. A raw `<script>` is kept only for page plumbing with no UI (the theme boot, the GTM loader), never to build markup. Logic stays in `src/lib/` as plain, tested functions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | The user asked for as much static content as possible, and for Svelte rather than hand-written scripts where the browser must render (2026-11-06). Static markup ships no JavaScript and is testable at build time; Svelte gives the client parts reactive templates, as Lit did, without hand-built DOM code.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Raw `<script>` tags filling static markup (hand-written DOM updates in every component); keeping Lit for the client parts; a heavier framework (React) for islands.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DD-173 | **The app shell is static Astro.** `AppLayout` (over `BaseLayout`) renders the header and the bottom bar at build time: `SiteHeader` and `TabBar` organisms, the `Tabs` molecule and the `Icon` atom. Each page names its tab (`<AppLayout tab="cards">`, so `/pay` is under Transactions, the card pages under Cards, Locations under Settings and the list pages under Home) and whether _+ New_ shows (`fab={false}` on the list pages, `/pay` and the card form). The build mark is static, from the build stamps. Labels are `data-i18n`, the navs' and buttons' names `data-i18n-aria-label`. Two parts need the browser: today's date is the first Svelte island (`HeaderDate`, `client:load`), and _+ New_'s menu is an HTML popover, which opens, closes on Escape or a tap outside and hands focus back with no script. `chrome.ts` keeps only the title, the theme and the build tint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | The shell is the same for every reader except the date, so it can ship as markup instead of being drawn by script on every load; the popover gives the menu's behaviour without JavaScript (DD-172).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | A Svelte island for _+ New_ (keeps focus on the first action and the arrow keys, but ships Svelte on every page for a two-link menu); keeping the date in the legacy shell.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| DD-174 | **Pages swap in place: Astro's ClientRouter.** `BaseLayout` renders `<ClientRouter />`, so a link or Back/Forward fetches the next page and swaps it in with a view transition instead of reloading. Addresses, `ENDPOINTS.md` and the static deploy are unchanged, and the language stays a reader setting (DD-171), not part of the address. A page's script runs only on its first visit, so it registers its route with `onPage("<route>", start)` (`#lib/ui/spa`), which starts the route on every visit whose `<main data-route>` names it. What a page sets up beyond its own DOM (a language listener, a `popstate` handler) passes `pageSignal()`, aborted as the reader leaves (`astro:before-swap`). A swap takes the next page's `<html>` attributes, so the boot script re-runs (`data-astro-rerun`) and `startCopy` re-applies on `astro:after-swap`; theme, tint and language never flash. The header, the bottom tab bar and _+ New_ don't animate, and the date island persists (`transition:persist`). The bar and _+ New_ are `position: fixed`, so each carries `transition:animate="none"` itself: naming their zero-height `#tabbar` slot held still an empty box and let both fade out with the page. Rewriting the address in place keeps the router's history state; a page's own in-page entry (an open sheet) carries none, so its Back stays the page's. Google Tag Manager sees only the first load of a visit: counting each page needs a History Change trigger in the container.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | The user asked for an SPA (2026-11-06) and chose Astro's ClientRouter over a single-page app, and DD-171 over Astro's i18n routing. It keeps every page a real, static address while navigation feels like an app.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | A single-page app with one `index.html` and a client router (every page rewritten as client routes, a Workers fallback, no static HTML per address); Astro's i18n routing (`/th/...`, rejected in DD-171).                                                                                                                                    |
| DD-175 | **The first page converted: Settings > Locations.** `/settings/locations` is `AppLayout` with the static `SubHeader` molecule and the `LocationsEditor` Svelte island in its `main` slot; the legacy route and its Lit rendering are gone. It looks and behaves as before (RD-17): the island keeps the legacy markup, so app.css draws it unchanged. Three pieces every converted page now shares: `openRepository` (`#lib/ui/startup`) opens the store and runs the startup migrations for islands and the legacy `bootstrap` alike; `titleKey` on `AppLayout` lets the copy pass translate `<title>` with the build's mark; and `ErrorBanner` is the light-DOM banner. The name rules moved to `#lib/ui/location-names`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | The smallest page first, to set the pattern every later page follows (PRD DD-172, DD-174) before the bigger ones.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Converting a bigger page first; keeping a Lit banner inside the island.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| DD-176 | **Settings converted.** `/settings` is `AppLayout` with a static title and the `SettingsPanels` Svelte island, which draws the five sections (RD-19) and holds what they share: the error banner, the Locations summary, the backup and reset rows, and their messages. Display and About need none of the reader's data, so the page passes them in as named slots: `DisplaySettings` (the language and theme switches, an island of its own) and `About`, static apart from the `BuiltTime` atom, which reads the build time in the viewer's zone. The two sheets are Svelte too: `RestoreSheet` and `PurgeSheet` over a new `Sheet` molecule, the light-DOM port of `cc-modal`'s sheet, which stays for the legacy pages. It looks and behaves as before; the backup file helpers moved to `#lib/ui/settings`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | One island for the sections that share state, so a restore re-reads the Locations summary and clears the reset message without islands talking to each other; slots keep the rest static (PRD DD-172).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | An island per section with a shared store; keeping the Lit sheets inside the island.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## Out of scope

Not built, on purpose. Adding any of these is a new decision.

- Personal spending; this tracks company purchases only.
- Charts, budgets, receipt uploads, bank statement import, notifications, splitting personal
  and company spending, multi-currency.
- Interest, fees and minimum payments; different limits for cards inside one shared group;
  a history of limit changes.
- Adding or renaming owners in the interface; a third language. _(Locations became editable
  in phase 6: RD-17.)_
- Edit, delete or undo in _This sitting_.

## Retired

Removed on purpose; listed so a redesign doesn't think they were lost.

- The dashboard's _Cards by location_ panel.
- A per-card _Can be used for new purchases_ checkbox, replaced by purchase places in
  Settings (DD-10).
- The language switch in the header (`cc-lang-switch`), replaced by _Display_ in Settings.
- Folding add and edit forms on the registry page, replaced by dialogs.
- The dashboard's Add a purchase dialog, replaced by the always-open form.
- Pico CSS, replaced by `@kcstyles/reset.css` and tokens (DD-31).
- The Cloudflare Worker with KV storage behind Cloudflare Access; the site is now static pages
  only.
- The page footer (FR-37), replaced by Settings › About and the build marks (RD-20).
- Backup format version 1.
- _Mark paid_ as one click that settled the whole statement, replaced by the payment dialog and
  partial payments (DD-37).
- The dashboard's purchase form, replaced by purchase lists (RD-09).
- _This sitting_ and _Start new batch_, replaced by waiting lists on Home (RD-15).
- _Can spend now_, replaced by _Ready to use_ (RD-15).
- _Due next_ and _Purchases by date_ on Home; they came back in Transactions (RD-13, phase 4).
- The old payment dialog (`payment-dialog.ts`, `cc-payment-form`), replaced by the page `/pay` (DD-76). The sheet mounted on every page (`pay-sheet.ts`, `mountPayHost`, the `cc:pay` event) and the `/pay` redirect of the first 2026-10-31 rework, replaced by the `/pay` picker page with its sheet.
- Per-part _Unmark_ and _Mark paid_ on the card page's statements, replaced by deleting the payment from Transactions (DD-77, DD-78).
- The version 2 backup download and copy (`transfer.ts`'s export), replaced by version 3 (DD-93); a version 2 file still restores.
- The registry page: its card and limit group dialogs, card table and limit group table, and the folds of the card list and the limit group list, replaced by the Cards page, the group sheet and the edit screen (DD-112 to DD-116).
- The `/cards` query view (DC-15) and the `/cards?edit=<id>` deep link (DC-16), replaced by DD-115 with no redirect (question 15).
- The card page's Add purchase dialog (`cc-quick-add`, `purchase-dialog.ts`), replaced by purchase lists; a purchase is edited from its statement row (DD-67, DD-111). _(Since 2026-11-01 a single purchase is added again from the card page's *Add purchase* sheet, DD-121.)_
- Settings' _Purchases_ section with its place checkboxes, replaced by _Takes purchases_ on each location at `/settings/locations` (RD-17); `settings.purchaseLocations` is read only to seed the records once (DD-128).
- Settings' side-by-side _Save a copy_ and _Restore a copy_ boxes, replaced by rows and the restore sheet (DD-134).
- Import writing `settings.purchaseLocations`, and export reading it (DD-94).
- The blank group sheet's `?group=new`, replaced by `?newGroup=1` (DD-137).
- The four fixed group pastels `--cc-group-1..4`, replaced by a colour stored on each limit group (DD-157).
- The word _Purchase_ or _Payment_ after the card on a ledger row (2026-11-04); the sign and the amount's colour say which (RD-13).
- Home's three-card _Ready to use_; it shows four (2026-11-04, CD-03).

## Acceptance criteria for the redesign

The redesign is done when all of these hold.

- [x] The open questions it depends on are answered and recorded here. _(Checked 2026-11-02: all 18.)_
- [ ] Every user story (US-01 to US-25) can be walked through in the new design.
- [ ] Every functional requirement is still met, or is retired here with a reason.
- [x] Domain rule tests in `src/lib/domain/` pass, and an expectation changed only where a recorded
  decision changed the rule (such as RD-11, payments to a card, and RD-17, location records).
  _(Amended and checked 2026-11-03.)_
- [x] The storage contract suite passes, and data saved by the current version loads unchanged.
  _(Checked 2026-11-03: `main-data.test.ts` opens localStorage as main 4876074 saved it.)_
- [x] A version 2 backup from the current version restores, with and without `settings`, and
  with payments both with and without `parts`.
- [ ] _(Dropped 2026-10-30, question 15.)_ `/cards` links with a query and `/cards?edit=<id>`
  still open the same view.
- [x] The delete guards in DR-19 still hold (DC-13). _(Checked 2026-11-02: the contract suite refuses both.)_
- [x] Every interface rule (UI-01 to UI-22) holds, checked in English and Thai, light and dark,
  at phone and desktop widths. _(Checked 2026-11-04: the controller checked screenshots of
      every changed screen at 390 and 1280, in English and Thai, light and dark, during phases 1
      to 7.)_
- [x] Each decision that changed has a dated entry in `CHANGELOG.md` saying what replaced it
  and why, and is updated here. _(Checked 2026-11-04: the entry "2026-11-04: The redesign
      ships".)_
- [ ] _(Dropped 2026-10-30, question 15.)_ Retired page addresses redirect.
- [x] A purchase list for three cards takes no more than: tick the cards, enter the amounts,
  review, mark sent; then _Made_ or _Not made_ per line and save (G3). _(Checked 2026-11-04:
      the user walked the main flows on the preview and approved them.)_
- [x] Payment records saved before RD-11 load as card payments, and every statement's paid
  and left amounts stay the same.
- [x] Every decided change (RD-03 to RD-21) is built, and the rows it names are updated or
  retired. _(Checked 2026-11-02: each row carries its phase.)_
- [x] The user has walked the main flows on the preview, on a phone and a desktop, and
  approved them. _(Checked 2026-11-04: the user approved on 2026-11-04.)_

## Constraints and known limits

- **Single browser.** Data is not shared between browsers or people; moving it means a backup.
  There are no accounts.
- **Storage cap.** Browsers allow `localStorage` about 5 MB per site, and a full store only shows
  up as a failed save.
- **No overpayment record.** Paying more than is left is accepted, but the extra is not kept as
  credit toward the next statement. _(Changed by RD-11: the extra becomes credit.)_
- **Shared limits depend on every card.** A group's room is only as right as the payments
  marked on every card in it; one card left unmarked understates the room on all of them.
- **Fixed sets.** Locations (Bangkok, Phichit, Krabi) and owners (KC, NT, RI) are closed lists
  in the code. _(Locations became editable in phase 6: RD-17. Owners stay closed.)_
- **Backup version 3.** Backups are written as version 3; a version 2 file converts on the way
  in, and a version 1 file is rejected (DC-05).
- **Restore is not atomic.** It checks shapes and ranges (DC-06, RD-06) but not that records
  refer to each other, and a failure partway can leave some records restored.
- **Thai sorting** uses code-unit order, not Thai collation; it matches for today's locations.
  _(Since phase 6 location names and the Cards name sort follow the reader's language, DD-132.)_
- **Small data.** Pages load every card and purchase; fine at today's size, worth revisiting if
  it grows.

## Risks

| Risk                                                                                                                    | What protects against it                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A layout change quietly changes a number (what's due, how much is left).                                                | Domain rules live in `src/lib/domain/` with tests; keep them unchanged (DR).                                                                                           |
| A refactor renames a storage key or field, and existing data stops loading.                                             | Data contracts (DC) and the storage contract suite; migrate on purpose or not at all.                                                                                  |
| Old backups stop restoring.                                                                                             | DC-05, DC-06 and DD-15; test a current backup against the new build.                                                                                                   |
| A shared link or the card page's _Edit card_ breaks.                                                                    | DC-15 and DC-16. _(Since 2026-11-04: both retired in phase 5, question 15; the old `/cards` links no longer work, by decision, and `/backup` still redirects, DC-17.)_ |
| Removing a delete button's condition lets a card with purchases be deleted, taking them with it.                        | DR-19 and DC-13; the storage layer refuses it too, and the contract suite checks it.                                                                                   |
| Thai is broken or untranslated in a new screen.                                                                         | UI-03 to UI-06; the catalog coverage test; screenshots in Thai.                                                                                                        |
| Replacing _This sitting_ drops the need with the mechanism, and the bookkeeper loses their place after an interruption. | G3, RD-02, US-08. Met in phase 3: waiting lists stay on Home until their reply is saved (RD-15).                                                                       |
| Stricter restore checks reject an old backup the user relies on.                                                        | RD-06 only rejects values the forms never allowed; check a real backup before shipping.                                                                                |
| A refactor drops the reading of old payment records without `parts`, and paid statements turn unpaid.                   | DR-29; the domain tests cover old records.                                                                                                                             |
| Moving payments from statements to cards changes what was paid or is left on an old statement.                          | RD-11 requires a migration that keeps every total; test it on a real backup.                                                                                           |
| A list waiting for the spender holds room and is forgotten.                                                             | Waiting lists stay at the top of Home and Transactions until saved (RD-13, RD-15).                                                                                     |
| Deleting old purchases changes what a card owes.                                                                        | RD-18 deletes only purchases on fully paid statements, after a count and a second confirmation.                                                                        |
| Something is dropped because no one knew it existed.                                                                    | This document; the [Retired](#retired) list says what was removed on purpose.                                                                                          |

## Open questions

To settle with the user before the redesign is planned. Record each answer here, and move it
into the matching section.

1. **What hurts today?** _Answered 2026-10-29_: a busy dashboard, paying buried, a clunky
   registry, and actions split across pages. See [Problem](#problem) and G6.
2. **What else is driving the redesign?** _Answered 2026-10-29_: the look and phone use, as well
   as the flows and the code. See [Problem](#problem).
3. **Pages.** Keep four pages (dashboard, cards, one card, settings), or reorganise? For
   example, should paying statements, entering receipts and checking room be separate places?
   _Answered 2026-10-30_: none of the three as drawn. Four pages, Home, Transactions, Cards and
   Settings, with + for payments (RD-08), after the user corrected how the app is used. The
   mockups first offered:
   - **A. Task tabs.** One place per task: Overview (read-only numbers), Add receipts, Pay
     (every unpaid statement across cards), Cards (registry, limit groups, card detail),
     Settings. Bottom tabs on a phone, top tabs on a desktop.
   - **B. Hub and sheets.** One Overview page; adding receipts, paying, editing a card and
     card history open as sheets over it and return to the same spot. Cards and Settings stay
     separate pages.
   - **C. Card-centric.** Overview lists cards; everything about one card (room, statements,
     paying, purchases, editing) lives on its page. Add receipts is its own screen. Paying
     several cards means visiting each.
4. **Entering receipts.** _Answered 2026-10-30_: entering receipts gets its own screen (RD-01).
   _This sitting_ doesn't fit as it is and is replaced (RD-02, question 11). _Superseded by
   RD-09: purchases come from purchase lists._
5. **Phone first?** _Answered 2026-10-29_: checking room and viewing the numbers. See G7.
6. **Look.** _Answered 2026-10-30_: start again (RD-03).
7. **Refactor depth.** _Answered 2026-10-30_: keep the same stack (RD-04).
8. **Theme flash.** _Answered 2026-10-30_: fix it (RD-05).
9. **Restore checks.** _Answered 2026-10-30_: check ranges (RD-06).
10. **Delete guards.** _Answered 2026-10-30_: move them into storage (RD-07).
11. **What replaces _This sitting_?** _Answered 2026-10-30_: a recent entries list on the entry
    screen, sorted by when each purchase was added (RD-02). _Superseded by RD-09: a list waiting
    for the spender's reply is how the bookkeeper keeps their place._
12. **Footer.** About in Settings now shows the build details (RD-20). Drop the page footer
    (FR-37), or keep a slim one? _Answered 2026-10-30_: drop it; About replaces it.
13. **A single purchase outside a list.** Is it still needed for an odd receipt, or does every
    purchase come from a list (RD-09)? _Answered 2026-10-30_: not needed; every purchase
    comes from a list. _Re-answered 2026-11-01, after testing the phase 5 preview_: needed. A
    single purchase can be added from the card page (DD-121); lists stay
    the way a planned purchase goes to the spender.
14. **Backup format.** Lists, planned payments, credit and the list of locations are new
    records. Add them as optional lists in version 2 (DD-15), or move to version 3 with a
    migration? _Answered 2026-10-30_: version 3. A version 2 backup still restores, migrated
    on the way in (G4).
15. **Page addresses.** Choose addresses for Transactions, a card, its edit screen and
    `/settings/locations`, and keep `/card?id=`, `/cards?…` and `/cards?edit=<id>` working
    (DC-15 to DC-17). _Answered 2026-10-30_: old links need not keep working, and retired
    addresses need no redirect. DC-15 and DC-16 retire with the redesign; DC-17 (the `/backup`
    redirect) stays. The new addresses are the builder's call.
16. **Credit on statements.** How credit shows on a card's statements and in _Owed_, and
    whether it can be refunded or moved to another card. _Answered 2026-10-30_: it stays on the
    card and its next purchases use it up; no refunds or moves.
17. **Delete old purchases.** Confirm the age choices (6 months, 1, 2, 3 years), and whether
    saved lists and payments that old go too (RD-18). _Answered 2026-10-30_: 6 months, 1 year,
    3 years, or all; lists and payments from the same time go too.
18. **Thai words** for the new terms in the glossary. _Answered 2026-10-30_: use them as written.

## Future work

Not part of this redesign.

- Show how much browser storage the app uses: a bar of bytes used against the cap on the
  settings page, split by kind (tracked in [`TODO.md`](TODO.md)).
- Shared storage across devices.
- Staff accounts. The earlier design expected sign-in through Cloudflare Access and a
  "recorded by" field on each purchase.

## Glossary

| Term               | Thai on screen        | Meaning                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------ | --------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Card               | บัตร                   | A physical company credit card, identified by an id the user picks.                                                                |
| Supplementary card | บัตรเสริม               | A card issued on someone else's account, with its own cardholder.                                                                  |
| Cardholder         | หน้าบัตร                | The person on the card (DR-13).                                                                                                    |
| Account holder     | เจ้าของบัตร             | The owner of a limit group's account: KC, NT or RI.                                                                                |
| Location           | สถานที่                 | Where a card is kept: Bangkok, Phichit or Krabi, editable from RD-17.                                                              |
| Limit group        | กลุ่มวงเงิน              | A pool of credit one or more cards draw on.                                                                                        |
| Available          | คงเหลือ                | A group's limit less everything unpaid (DR-09).                                                                                    |
| Billing cycle      | รอบบิล                 | A card's rule for its close and due dates (DR-04).                                                                                 |
| Statement          | ใบแจ้งยอด              | One period's purchases on one card, worked out from the cycle (DR-03, DR-05).                                                      |
| Period             | รอบ                   | A statement's month, `YYYY-MM`, named after its close date.                                                                        |
| Close date         | วันสรุปยอด              | The last day of a statement period.                                                                                                |
| Due date           | วันครบกำหนด            | When a statement must be paid.                                                                                                     |
| Open period        | รอบปัจจุบัน              | The statement a purchase made today lands on (DR-08); marked _Current_.                                                            |
| Due next           | ครบกำหนดถัดไป          | The statement that needs paying next on a card (DR-06).                                                                            |
| Mark paid          | บันทึกว่าชำระแล้ว         | Record a payment toward a closed statement; it is paid once payments cover the total (DR-28).                                      |
| Payment part       | ชำระเงิน               | One payment toward a statement: an amount and the date paid.                                                                       |
| Left to pay        | ยอดคงเหลือ             | A statement's total less what was paid, never below zero (DR-28).                                                                  |
| Unmark             | ยกเลิกชำระ             | Remove one payment from a statement (DR-30).                                                                                       |
| Archived           | เก็บเข้าคลัง             | A retired card: hidden from daily views, history kept, still counts against its group.                                             |
| Purchase places    | สถานที่ที่บันทึกรายการใหม่ได้ | The locations whose cards can take a new purchase (DR-12); since phase 6, the locations ticked _Takes purchases_ (บันทึกรายการซื้อได้). |
| This sitting       | รอบนี้                  | _(Retired in phase 3.)_ Purchases entered since _Start new batch_ was last pressed (DR-16).                                        |
| Batch start        | เวลาเริ่มรอบ            | _(Retired in phase 3.)_ When the current sitting began.                                                                            |
| Entry time         | เวลาที่เพิ่ม              | When a purchase was first entered (`addedAt`), as opposed to its purchase date.                                                    |
| Satang             | สตางค์                 | 1/100 baht; how money is stored (DR-01).                                                                                           |
| Purchase list      | ใบรายการซื้อ            | A dated list of cards and amounts sent to the spender before buying (RD-09).                                                       |
| Spender's reply    | คำตอบของผู้ใช้บัตร        | Which lines on a list were made, with the real amounts.                                                                            |
| Ready to use       | พร้อมใช้                | Cards in places that take purchases, shown on Home to pick for a list (RD-15).                                                     |
| Planned payment    | การชำระที่วางแผนไว้      | A payment dated later than today; it counts once marked paid (RD-12).                                                              |
| Credit             | เครดิต                 | Money paid beyond what a card owes, used by its next purchases (RD-11).                                                            |
| Upcoming           | กำลังจะมาถึง            | Waiting lists and planned payments, not counted yet (RD-13).                                                                       |
| Transactions       | รายการเคลื่อนไหว        | The ledger of purchases (−) and payments (+) (RD-13).                                                                              |
| Environment        | สภาพแวดล้อม            | Which build is running: development, local, preview or production (RD-20).                                                         |
